<script type="application/javascript">
  if (!String.prototype.endsWith) {
    String.prototype.endsWith = function (searchString, position) {
      var subjectString = this.toString();
      if (typeof position !== 'number' || !isFinite(position) || Math.floor(position) !== position || position > subjectString.length) {
        position = subjectString.length;
      }
      position -= searchString.length;
      var lastIndex = subjectString.indexOf(searchString, position);
      return lastIndex !== -1 && lastIndex === position;
    };
  }

  window.Rainfocus = window.Rainfocus || {};

  var Loader = function () {}
  Loader.prototype = {
    require: function (scriptMaps, callback) {
      this.loadCount = 0;
      this.totalRequired = scriptMaps.length;
      this.callback = callback;

      for (var i = 0; i < scriptMaps.length; i++) {
        this.writeScript(scriptMaps[i]);
      }
    },
    loaded: function (evt) {
      this.loadCount++;
      if (this.loadCount === this.totalRequired && typeof this.callback === 'function') this.callback.call();
    },
    writeScript: function (script) {
      const isScriptMap = typeof script === 'object';
      const src = isScriptMap ? script.src : script;
      var self = this;
      var isJavaScript = src.endsWith('.js');

      var s = isJavaScript ? document.createElement('script') : document.createElement('link');
      if (isJavaScript) {
        s.type = "text/javascript";
        s.async = true;
        s.src = src;
      } else {
        s.setAttribute('rel', 'stylesheet');
        s.type = 'text/css';
        s.href = src;
      }
      if (isScriptMap && script.integrity) {
        s.setAttribute('integrity', script.integrity)
        s.setAttribute('crossorigin', 'anonymous')
      }
      s.addEventListener('load', function (e) {
        self.loaded(e);
      }, false);
      var head = document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0];
      head.appendChild(s);
    }
  };

  function checkStatus(response) {
    return response.json().then(function (data) {
      var newResponse = {};
      for (var k in data) {
        if (data.hasOwnProperty(k))
          newResponse[k] = data[k];
      }
      newResponse['status'] = response.status;
      newResponse['ok'] = response.ok;
      if (response.status >= 200 && response.status < 300 && response.status !== 210) {
        return newResponse
      } else {
        throw newResponse
      }
    })
  }

  /**
   * All functions in this util are exposed for client use in analytics config.
   * Any updates to these functions or their underlying implementation should be carefully considered with backwards compatibility in mind.
   */
  window.Rainfocus.Utils = {
    decodeEntities: function (encodedString) {
      var textArea = document.createElement('textarea');
      textArea.innerHTML = encodedString;
      return textArea.value;
    },
    loadInlineScript: function (content, target) {
      var newScript = document.createElement("script");
      var inlineScript = document.createTextNode(content);
      newScript.appendChild(inlineScript);
      target.appendChild(newScript);
      return newScript;
    },
    loadURLScripts: function (scriptMaps, callback) {
      var l = new Loader();
      l.require(scriptMaps, callback)
    },
    split: function (value, separator) {
      if (value === null || value === undefined)
        return [""];
      return value.split(separator);
    },
    arrayToParam: function (key, value) {
      if (value === null && value === undefined && value.length === 0)
        return '';

      return value.map(function (val) {
        return key + '=' + val;
      }).join('&')
    },
    doGet: function (url, handler) {
      if(window.version) {
        url += (url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?') + "ver=" + window.version
      }
      if (window.fetch) {
        window.fetch(
          url,
          {
            method: 'get',
            credentials: 'include',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'
            }
          }
        )
        .then(checkStatus)
        .then(handler)
      } else {
        if (!$ || !$.ajax)
          console.error("IMPORTANT!!!!!: Please include jquery ajax in branding footer for analytics to work");
        else
          $.ajax({
            url: url,
            method: 'GET',
            contentType: 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'
          })
          .done(handler);
      }
    },
    executeScriptWithData: function (script, data) {
      if (script === null || script === undefined || script === '' || script.trim() === '')
        return;
      return new Function('data', script)(data);
    },
    domReady: function (callback) {
      document.readyState === 'interactive' || document.readyState === 'complete'
          ? callback()
          : document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', callback);
    },
    
    waitForX: function waitForX(options) {
      var log = function log() {
        if (options.enableLogs)
          console.log.apply(console, arguments)
      }

      var logSuffix = options.logIdentifier ? ' -- [' + options.logIdentifier + ']' : ''
      var intervalFrequency = options.intervalFrequency || 100
      var maxIterations = options.maxIterations || 100

      // Do 1 immediate check before the interval starts.
      if (options.successCheck()) {
        log('SUCCESS (immediate), no interval needed' + logSuffix)
        options.onSuccess()
        return;
      }

      log('every ' + intervalFrequency + 'ms, for ' + intervalFrequency * maxIterations + 'ms' + logSuffix)
      var iterations = 0
      var waitTimer = window.setInterval(function () {
        if (options.successCheck.apply(options)) {
          log('SUCCESS, waited: ' + ((iterations + 1) * intervalFrequency) + 'ms (' + (iterations + 1) + ' iterations)' + logSuffix)
          options.onSuccess.apply(options)

          window.clearInterval(waitTimer);
          log('cleared interval' + logSuffix)
        }
        if (iterations >= maxIterations) {
          // Safety net to ensure this interval won't run forever.
          log('TIMED OUT' + logSuffix)
          window.clearInterval(waitTimer)
          log('cleared interval' + logSuffix)

          if (options.onTimeout) {
            options.onTimeout.apply(options)
          }
        }
        iterations++
      }, options.intervalFrequency)
    }
  };

  window.Rainfocus.Analytics = (function (Utils) {
    var textProperties = JSON.parse('{"cookie.consent.button.text":"#444","cookie.consent.message.implied":"This site uses cookies to provide you with a more responsive and personalized service. If you continue to use this site, you consent to our use of cookies.","cookie.consent.message.explicit":"This site uses cookies to provide you with a more responsive and personalized service. By using this site and clicking on the \\"Accept\\" button, you agree to our use of cookies. Please read our Privacy Policy for more information on the types of cookies we use and how to delete or block them.","cookie.consent.dismiss":"Dismiss","cookie.consent.allow":"Accept","cookie.consent.deny":"Decline","cookie.consent.href":"https://static.rainfocus.com/rainfocus/public/rainfocus-privacy-policy.pdf","cookie.consent.link":"Learn more","cookie.consent.button.background":"#fff","cookie.consent.close":"\\u0026#x274c;","cookie.consent.popup.text":"#fff","cookie.consent.policy":"Cookie Policy","cookie.consent.header":"Website Cookies","cookie.consent.popup.background":"#444"}');

    function getTextProperty(key, defaultValue) {
      return textProperties[key] || defaultValue;
    }

    var GDPRScript;
    
    
    

    var configs = [];

    return {
      initScripts: function () {
        configs.forEach(function (config) {
          if (!config.initialized) {
            config.init();
          }
        });
      },
      addConfig: function (config) {
        configs.push(config);
      },
      initGDPRScript: function () {
        GDPRScript.init();
      },
      executeProcessScriptsWithData: function (data) {
        configs.forEach(function (config) {
          if (config.initialized) {
            Utils.executeScriptWithData(config.processScript, data.configs[config.id]);
          }
        });
      },
      run: function () {
        if (GDPRScript) {
          this.initGDPRScript();
        } else {
          this.initScripts();
        }

        
        window.Rainfocus.Utils.waitForX({
          successCheck: function () {return window.EventBus; },
          onSuccess: function () {
            EventBus.on('rainfocus:location.change', function (path) {
              var app = 'workflow';
              
              if (typeof path !== 'string') {
                app = path.app;
                path = path.path;
              }

              // When navigation occurs, re-run the process script.
              var elemPaths = Utils.split(path, "/");
              var page = elemPaths[elemPaths.length - 1];

              if (configs.length > 0) {
                var param = Utils.arrayToParam('configIds', configs.map(function (c) {
                  return c.id
                }));

                var baseUrl = (window.activeFeatures && window.activeFeatures.indexOf('WORKFLOW_UNAUTH_AJAX_CDN') > -1 && window.store && !window.store.getState().attendee) && window.baseCdnUrl ? window.baseCdnUrl : window.ajaxBaseAppURL
                var url = baseUrl + 'flow/loadAnalyticsData?pageUri=' + page + '&' + param + (window.workflowApiToken ? '&workflowApiToken=' + window.workflowApiToken : '');

                Utils.doGet(url, function (response) {
                  var data = response.data || {};

                  if (data && (data.responseCode === '0') && ((data.configs !== null) || (data.configs !== undefined))) {
                    configs.forEach(function (config) {
                      var scriptData = data.configs[config.id] || {};
                      scriptData.page = page;
                      // The "app" field will allow our ACs to conditionally fire/suppress analytics based on which app's url is changing.
                      scriptData.app = app;
                      config.runProcessScript(scriptData);
                    });
                  }
                });
              }
            });
          },
          intervalFrequency: 10,
          maxIterations: 15000,
        })

        
      }
    }
  })(window.Rainfocus.Utils);
</script>

  <script id="provider_script_1789388027753002TsyX" type="text/plain">
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

    
    // Create the banner only if not already found
    if (!document.getElementById('msccBannerV2')) {
        const banner = document.createElement('div');
        banner.id = 'msccBannerV2';
        document.body.prepend(banner);
        console.log("msccBannerV2 created");
    }

    // Function to load script
    function loadScript(url, isAsync = false) {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.src = url;
            script.async = isAsync;

            script.onload = () => {
                console.log(`Script loaded: ${url}`);
                resolve();
            };

            script.onerror = () => {
                console.error(`Error loading: ${url}`);
                reject(new Error(`Load error: ${url}`));
            };

            document.head.appendChild(script);
        });
    }

    // Load all scripts in order
    async function loadAllScripts(scripts) {
        console.log('Loading WCP Consent + OneDS sequentially...');
        for (const script of scripts) {
          await loadScript(script.url, script.async);
        }
        console.log('All consent scripts loaded.');
    }

   
    const scriptsInOrder = [

  <script id="process_script_1789388027753002TsyX" type="text/plain">
    
// ================== CONFIG ==================

const CONFIG = {
  ENDPOINT: '/flow/updateAttendee',
  apiProfile: '0QvqfXM1qYXGmUNtfSPwVOrbJmWTxcAB',
  formId: '1758922507332001e2uv',
  STATUS: {
    STARTED: 'Started',
    COMPLETED: 'Completed',
    ATTRIBUTE_CODE: 'registrationStatus'
  },
  REG: {
    INIT_ATTRIBUTE_CODE: 'wt.mc._id',
    COMPLETE_ATTRIBUTE_CODE: 'ocid=',
  }
};

// ================== GLOBALS ==================
const attendeeId = data?.attendee?.attendeeId;
const eventCode = data?.event?.code;
const registrationStatus = data?.attendee?.registrationStatus;
// ❗ IMPORTANT: Please make sure this event attribute has been exposed.
const regIkey = data?.event?.regIkey
const page = data?.page || '';
let mpsonedsanalytics;
var siteConsent;

// ================== HELPERS ==================
function log(text, type = 0, ) {
  const colors = ["#50C878", "#FF4C4C", "#FFA500"];
  const color = colors[type] || colors[0];
  console.log(`%c${text}`, `color: ${color}; font-weight: bold;`);
}

const missingRequiredAttributes = () => {
  return [registrationStatus, attendeeId, eventCode].some(
    v => v === undefined
  );
};

const saveRegistrationData = async (attributes) => {
  if(!eventCode || !attendeeId){
    log("Cannot update registration status: eventCode or attendeeId is empty.",1);
    return;
  }

  const url = `${CONFIG.ENDPOINT}?apiProfile=${CONFIG.apiProfile}&formId=${CONFIG.formId}`;

  const payload = {
    attendeeId,
    event: eventCode,
    ...attributes
  };

  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json'
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  })

  const { data } = await response.json();

  if (data.responseCode !== "0")
    throw new Error(`API error, code: ${data.responseCode}, message: ${data.responseMessage}`);

  return data;
}

const getCampaignCode = () => {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem('MSFTCampaignCode')
  } catch (e) {
    log("Error accessing localStorage", 2);
    return null;
  }
};

// ================== HANDLERS ==================
const handleRegInit = () => {
  if(page === 'digitalreg'){
    if (!attendeeId || !eventCode || registrationStatus === undefined) {
      log("REG INIT NOT FIRING: Missing required attributes", 1);
      return;
    }

    if(![CONFIG.STATUS.STARTED, CONFIG.STATUS.COMPLETED].includes(registrationStatus)){
      const attributesToUpdate = {
        [CONFIG.STATUS.ATTRIBUTE_CODE]: CONFIG.STATUS.STARTED
      }

      const campaignCode = getCampaignCode();

      if (campaignCode) {
        attributesToUpdate[CONFIG.REG.INIT_ATTRIBUTE_CODE] = campaignCode;
      }
      
      saveRegistrationData(attributesToUpdate)
        .then(() => log("Success: Attributes updated successfully."))
        .catch((err) => console.error("Failed to save registration status:", err));

      log("FIRE REG INIT: Started")
      captureRegInit(mpsonedsanalytics, attendeeId)
    } else{
      log(`REG INIT NOT FIRING, registration status: ${registrationStatus}`,2)
    }
  }
}

const handleRegComplete = () => {
  if(page === 'confirmation' || page === 'landing'){
    if (!attendeeId || !eventCode || registrationStatus === undefined) {
      log("REG COMPLETE NOT FIRING: Missing required attributes", 1);
      return;
    }
    if(registrationStatus === CONFIG.STATUS.STARTED){
      const attributesToUpdate = {
        [CONFIG.STATUS.ATTRIBUTE_CODE]: CONFIG.STATUS.COMPLETED
      }

      const campaignCode = getCampaignCode();

      if (campaignCode) {
        attributesToUpdate[CONFIG.REG.COMPLETE_ATTRIBUTE_CODE] = campaignCode;
      }

      saveRegistrationData(attributesToUpdate)
        .then(() => console.log("Success: Registration status updated successfully."))
        .catch((err) => console.error("Failed to save registration status:", err));

      log(`FIRE REG COMPLETE, prev registration status: ${registrationStatus}`)
      captureRegComplete(mpsonedsanalytics, attendeeId);
    }else{
      log(`REG COMPLETE NOT FIRING, registration status: ${registrationStatus}`,2)
    }
  }
}

// ================== ANALYTICS ==================

//Reg Initiation: Function to send analytics data
function captureRegInit(mpsonedsanalytics, registrantId) {
  console.log('mpsonedsanalytics: ', mpsonedsanalytics)
  var overrideValues = {
    behavior: "REGISTRATIONINITIATE",
    actionType: "CL",
    contentTags: {
      field1: "1",
      field3: registrantId
    }
  };
  let result = mpsonedsanalytics.capturePageAction(null, overrideValues);
}

//Reg Completion: Function to send analytics data
function captureRegComplete(mpsonedsanalytics, registrantId) {
  var overrideValues = {
    behavior: "REGISTRATIONCOMPLETE",
    actionType: "CL",
    contentTags: {
      field1: "1",
      field2: registrantId
    }
  };
  let result = mpsonedsanalytics.capturePageAction(null, overrideValues);
}

function loadClarity(consent) {
  if (!consent) return;


  // Load Clarity only once
  if (!window.__clarity_loaded) {
      window.__clarity_loaded = true;
    (function (c, l, a, r, i, t, y) {
      c[a] = c[a] || function () {
        (c[a].q = c[a].q || []).push(arguments);
      };
      t = l.createElement(r);
      t.async = 1;
      t.src = 'https://www.clarity.ms/tag/' + i;

      y = l.getElementsByTagName(r)[0];
      y.parentNode.insertBefore(t, y);
    })(window, document, 'clarity', 'script', 'vuh5kayq26');
  }


  // Apply MSCC consent to Clarity
  if (typeof window.clarity === 'function') {
    window.clarity('consentv2', {
      analytics_storage: consent.Analytics ? 'granted' : 'denied',
      ad_storage: consent.Advertising ? 'granted' : 'denied'
    });

    //  console.log("Clarity consent applied:", consent);
  }
}


const loadMpsonedsAnalytics = () => {
  mpsonedsanalytics = new oneDS.ApplicationInsights();

  var isLoggedIn = window?.initialState?.isAttendeeLoggedIn || false

// Helper function to get a specific cookie value by name (Eg: 'MSCC')
  function getCookie(cookieName) {
    const cookieValue = document.cookie.match('(^|;)\\s*' + cookieName + '\\s*=\\s*([^;]+)');
    return cookieValue ? cookieValue[2] : '';
  }

// Function to check user's consent preferences from the MSCC cookie
  function checkConsents() {

    const WcpConsentCookieName = 'MSCC';
    const consentsParams = getCookie(WcpConsentCookieName);

    //For Non-banner countries, default all consents values to true
    // For non-banner countries OR when cookie = "NR", default all consents to TRUE
    if (!consentsParams || consentsParams === "NR") {
      return {
        Required: true,
        Analytics: true,
        SocialMedia: true,
        Advertising: true
      };
    }


    //For banner countries, pass the value as per the user preferences
    const params = new URLSearchParams(consentsParams.replaceAll('-', '&'));

    return {
      Analytics: params.get('c1') === '2',
      SocialMedia: params.get('c2') === '2',
      Advertising: params.get('c3') === '2',
      Required: true
    };
  }

// New variable for initial consents
  var initialConsents = checkConsents();
  // loadClarity(initialConsents);
  siteConsent = null;

  if (typeof (GPC_DataSharingOptIn) === 'undefined') {
    const globalPrivacyControlEnabled = navigator.globalPrivacyControl;
    const gpc_DataSharingOptIn_Value = (globalPrivacyControlEnabled) ? false : checkThirdPartyAdsOptOutCookie();
    GPC_DataSharingOptIn = gpc_DataSharingOptIn_Value;
  }
  function checkThirdPartyAdsOptOutCookie() {

    try {
      const ThirdPartyAdsOptOutCookieName = '3PAdsOptOut';
      var cookieValue = getCookie(ThirdPartyAdsOptOutCookieName);
      return cookieValue != 1;
    } catch {
      return true;
    }
  }

  var config = {
    instrumentationKey: regIkey, //change it per event based on event config
    channelConfiguration: { eventsLimitInMem: 50 },
    propertyConfiguration: {
      gpcDataSharingOptIn: GPC_DataSharingOptIn,
      callback: {
        userConsentDetails: function () {
          try {
            // If MSCC/WCP has already provided consent → use that
            if (siteConsent && siteConsent.getConsent) {
              return siteConsent.getConsent();
            }
          } catch (e) {}
          // Otherwise use the initial cookie-based values
          return initialConsents;
        }

      },
    },
    webAnalyticsConfiguration: { // Web Analytics Plugin configuration
      autoCapture: {
        scroll: true,
        pageView: false,
        onLoad: true,
        onUnload: true,
        click: true,
        resize: true,
        jsError: true,
      },
      urlCollectQuery: true,
      urlCollectHash: true,
      isLoggedIn
    }
  };

// Initialize SDK
  mpsonedsanalytics.initialize(config, []);

// Set gpcDataSharingOptIn property
  mpsonedsanalytics.getPropertyManager().getPropertiesContext().web.gpcDataSharingOptIn = GPC_DataSharingOptIn;

// Function to initialize WCP Consent when available
  function checkWcpConsentInit() {
    try {
      // Check if the WCP Consent library is loaded
      if (window.WcpConsent) {
        // Detect user's browser language (fallback to English if not found)
        var userLang = navigator.language || navigator.userLanguage || 'en';
        window.WcpConsent.init(
          userLang,     // language
          'msccBannerV2',    // banner id - default Microsoft consent banner

          //Callback 1: Runs after consent initialization
          function (err, consent) {
            if (!err && consent) {
              // Store consent object reference globally
              siteConsent = consent;
              siteConsent._ready = true;
              // Get user's current consent categories (Analytics, Ads, etc.)
              const consentCategories = consent.getConsent();
              if (consentCategories) {
                const webProps = mpsonedsanalytics.getPropertyManager().getPropertiesContext().web;
                webProps.Required = consentCategories.Required;
                webProps.Analytics = consentCategories.Analytics;
                webProps.SocialMedia = consentCategories.SocialMedia;
                webProps.Advertising = consentCategories.Advertising;

                loadClarity(consentCategories);
              }
            } else {
              console.error('Error initializing WcpConsent:', err);
            }
          },
          // Callback 2: Runs whenever user updates their consent preferences
          function (newConsentCategories) {
            //  Update telemetry properties in real-time when user changes consent

            const webProps = mpsonedsanalytics.getPropertyManager().getPropertiesContext().web;
            webProps.Required = newConsentCategories.Required;
            webProps.Analytics = newConsentCategories.Analytics;
            webProps.SocialMedia = newConsentCategories.SocialMedia;
            webProps.Advertising = newConsentCategories.Advertising;

            loadClarity(newConsentCategories);
          },
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error initializing WcpConsent:', err);
    }
  }


// Timer to check every 100ms if WCP Consent library has loaded
  var wcpConsentTimer = setInterval(() => {
    try {
      if (window.WcpConsent && document.getElementById('msccBannerV2')){
        clearInterval(wcpConsentTimer);
        checkWcpConsentInit();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  }, 100);

// Delay page view tracking by 2 seconds
  setTimeout(() => {
    // Send a page view event to OneDS (Microsoft analytics)
    mpsonedsanalytics.trackPageView({
      name: document.title,
      uri: window.location.href
    });
  }, 2000);
}
// Waits for WCP consent to be ready, or resolves immediately for non-banner countries.

function waitForConsentReady() {
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const check = () => {
      if (siteConsent && siteConsent._ready) {
        resolve(true);  // real MSCC/WCP consent received
      } else if (!window.WcpConsent) {
        // Non-banner countries: WCP won't load → use initialConsents
        resolve(true);
      } else {
        setTimeout(check, 50);
      }
    };
    check();
  });
}

// }

loadMpsonedsAnalytics();
waitForConsentReady().then(() => {
  handleRegInit();
  handleRegComplete();
});


//CampaignCode Implementation

function getQueryParam(paramName) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  
  for (const [key, value] of params.entries()) {
    if (key.toLowerCase() === paramName.toLowerCase()) {
      return value;
    }
  }
  
  return null;
}

// Get values
const wtMcId = getQueryParam("wt.mc_id");
const ocid = getQueryParam("ocid");

// Priority logic
if (wtMcId && wtMcId.trim() !== "") {
  localStorage.setItem("MSFTCampaignCode", wtMcId);
} else if (ocid && ocid.trim() !== "") {
  localStorage.setItem("MSFTCampaignCode", ocid);
}

  <script type="application/javascript">
    var Analytics = window.Rainfocus.Analytics;
    var Utils = window.Rainfocus.Utils;

    Analytics.addConfig((function () {
      var configId = '1789388027753002TsyX';
      var initScript = Utils.decodeEntities(document.getElementById('init_script_' + configId).innerHTML);
      var providerScript = Utils.decodeEntities(document.getElementById('provider_script_' + configId).innerHTML);
      var processScript = Utils.decodeEntities(document.getElementById('process_script_' + configId).innerHTML);
      
      var listenersScript = Utils.decodeEntities(document.getElementById('listeners_script_' + configId).innerHTML);
      

      var head = window.document.head;

      var savedDataForLaterRun = null;

      return {
        id: configId,
        initialized: false,
        type: 'Custom',
        init: function () {
          
          if (initScript.replace(/\s/g, ''))
            Utils.loadInlineScript(initScript, head);
          if (providerScript.replace(/\s/g, ''))
            Utils.loadInlineScript(providerScript, head);

          this.initialized = true;
          if (savedDataForLaterRun) {
            this.runProcessScript(savedDataForLaterRun);
            savedDataForLaterRun = null;
          }
          
          if (listenersScript.replace(/\s/g, ''))
            Utils.loadInlineScript(listenersScript, head);
          
        },
        runProcessScript: function (data) {
          if (this.initialized)
            Utils.executeScriptWithData(processScript, data);
          else
            savedDataForLaterRun = data;
        }
      }
    })());
  </script>

    console.log("Microsoft | Scale events 1DS Tagging Update")
const page = data?.page;
const getRegistrantId = () => data?.attendee?.attendeeId || null;

/* -------------------------------- */
/* Constants */
/* -------------------------------- */

const PAGES = Object.freeze({
  LANDING: 'landing',
  SESSIONS: 'sessions',
  DIGITAL_REG: 'digitalreg',
});

const SESSION_DETAIL_REGEX = /\/page\/sessions\/[^/]+\/[^/]+$/;

const SOCIALS = Object.freeze({
  linkedin: ['linkedin.com', 'linkedin'],
  facebook: ['facebook.com', 'facebook'],
  twitter: ['twitter.com', 'x.com', 'twitter', 'x'],
  instagram: ['instagram.com', 'instagram'],
  youtube: ['youtube.com', 'youtube'],
});

/* -------------------------------- */
/* DOM Helpers */
/* -------------------------------- */

const $ = (selector, parent = document) => parent?.querySelector(selector) || null;

const $$ = (selector, parent = document) => parent ? [...parent.querySelectorAll(selector)] : [];

const safeText = (element) => element?.textContent?.trim() || '';

const normalizeText = (text = '') => text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();

const capitalize = (value = '') => value.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + value.slice(1).toLowerCase();

const isSessionDetailPage = () => SESSION_DETAIL_REGEX.test(window.location.pathname);

/* -------------------------------- */
/* Analytics Helpers */
/* -------------------------------- */

const getDataM = (element) => {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(element?.getAttribute('data-m') || '{}');
  } catch { return {} }
};

const setDataM = (element, values = {}) => {
  if (!element) return;

  const current = getDataM(element);

  element.setAttribute('data-m', JSON.stringify({ ...current, ...values,}));
};

const setTarget = (element) => {
  if (element?.href) {
    element.setAttribute('data-target', element.href);
  }
};

const updateAnalytics = (element, { aN = '', cN = '', withTarget = false} = {},) => {
  if (!element) return;

  const base = { aN, cN };

  const field1 = getRegistrantId();
  if (field1)
    base.field1 = field1;

  setDataM(element, base);

  if (withTarget) {
    setTarget(element);
  }
};

/* -------------------------------- */
/* Utility Helpers */
/* -------------------------------- */

const detectSocialMedia = (element) => {
  if (!element) return null;

  const searchableText = [
    element.href,
    element.className,
    element.getAttribute('aria-label'),
    element.getAttribute('title'),
    element.textContent,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' ')
    .toLowerCase();

  for (const [social, patterns] of Object.entries(SOCIALS)) {
    if ( patterns.some((pattern) => searchableText.includes(pattern)) ) {
      return capitalize(social);
    }
  }

  return null;
};

const getAnchorTagByText = (text, parent = document) => $$('a', parent).find( (anchor) => normalizeText(anchor.textContent) === normalizeText(text) );

const findHeading = ({ selector = 'h2', text}) =>
  $$(selector).find( (element) => normalizeText(element.textContent) === normalizeText(text),);

const bindExpandableAnalytics = ({ button, aN, subTopic }) => {
  if (!button) return;

  const setState = (expanded) => {
    updateAnalytics(button, {
      aN,
      cN: `${subTopic}_${expanded ? 'Expanded' : 'Collapsed'}`,
    });
  };

  // initial state
  setState(button.matches('[aria-expanded="true"]'));

  button.addEventListener(
    'pointerdown',
    () => {
      const expanded =
        button.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';

      // pre-update BEFORE click logic & telemetry
      setState(!expanded);

      console.log(
        'PRE-UPDATED:',
        button.getAttribute('data-m')
      );
    },
    true
  );
};

const handleLandingPage = () => {
  const heroTitle = safeText(
    $('.ms-hero__title'),
  );

  const agendaTitle = safeText(
    $('.ms-agenda-section__title'),
  );

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 1. Logo */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const logo = $('.uhf-site-logo');

  updateAnalytics(logo, {
    aN: 'Navigation',
    cN: 'Microsoft events',
    withTarget: true,
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* All Microsoft Button */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const allMicrosoftBtn = $$('button').find(
    (button) => {
      return [...button.childNodes].some(
        (node) =>
          node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE &&
          node.textContent.trim() ===
          'All Microsoft',
      );
    },
  );

  updateAnalytics(allMicrosoftBtn, {
    aN: 'Navigation',
    cN: 'All Microsoft',
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 2 / 5 / 6 Register Buttons */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  $$(
    'a.ms-button--primary, a.ms-button--secondary',
  ).forEach((button) => {
    updateAnalytics(button, {
      aN: heroTitle,
      cN: `${safeText(button)}_CTA`,
      withTarget: true,
    });
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 7. Navigation Buttons */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  $$('ul.ms-ip-nav__links button').forEach(
    (button) => {
      updateAnalytics(button, {
        aN: 'Navigation',
        cN: safeText(button),
      });
    },
  );

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 8. Nav Register Button */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const navRegisterBtn = $('.ms-ip-nav__inner-right a.ms-button--primary');

  updateAnalytics(navRegisterBtn, {
    aN: 'Microsoft events',
    cN: `${safeText(navRegisterBtn)}_CTA`,
    withTarget: true,
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 9. Overview Link */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const overviewLink = $('.ms-overview__intro .ms-overview__intro-content p > a');

  updateAnalytics(overviewLink, {
    aN: 'Overview',
    cN: safeText(overviewLink),
    withTarget: true,
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 10. Share Button */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const overviewContainer = $('.ms-overview__details .ms-event-detail');

  if (overviewContainer) {
    // const aN = safeText($('.ms-event-detail__content h3', overviewContainer));
    const aN = 'Microsoft events'

    const shareButton = $('.ms-event-detail__actions button.ms-button', overviewContainer,);

    updateAnalytics(shareButton, {
      aN,
      cN: safeText(shareButton),
    });

    /* ---------------------------- */
    /*. Share this event */
    /* ---------------------------- */
    shareButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        const modal = $('.ms-modal');
        if (!modal) return;

        const aN = safeText($('.ms-modal__header .ms-modal__title', modal));

        const elements = $$('.ms-modal__link-group :is(button, a)', modal);

        elements.forEach((element) => {
          updateAnalytics(element, {
            aN,
            cN: safeText(element),
          });
        });

        const closeButton = $('.ms-modal__close button[aria-label="Close"]', modal);

        if (closeButton) {
          updateAnalytics(closeButton, {
            aN,
            cN: 'Close',
          });
        }
      });
    });

  }

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 11. View Sessions Button */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const viewSessionsBtn = $('.ms-agenda-section__action a.ms-button');

  updateAnalytics(viewSessionsBtn, {
    aN: agendaTitle,
    cN: `${safeText(viewSessionsBtn)}_CTA`,
    withTarget: true,
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 12. Agenda Pills */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  $$('.ms-pill-bar button[role="tab"]').forEach((pill) => {
    updateAnalytics(pill, {
      aN: agendaTitle,
      cN: safeText(pill),
    });
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 13. Session Links */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  $$('a.ms-agenda__link').forEach((link) => {
    const sessionTitle = safeText(
      $('.ms-agenda__heading',
        link.closest('.ms-agenda__textblock')),
    );

    updateAnalytics(link, {
      aN: agendaTitle,
      cN: `${sessionTitle}_${safeText(link)}`,
      withTarget: true,
    });
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 22. Speakers */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const speakersComponent = $('ms-speakers');

  if (speakersComponent) {
    const aN = safeText($('h2', speakersComponent));

    $$('.ms-speaker__text', speakersComponent).forEach(
      (speaker) => {
        const link = $('div > a', speaker);

        if (!link) return;

        const speakerName = safeText(
          $('.ms-speaker__details h3', speaker),
        );

        const social =
          detectSocialMedia(link);

        updateAnalytics(link, {
          aN,
          cN: `${speakerName}_${
            social || ''
          }_CTA`,
        });
      },
    );
  }

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 23. Additional Details */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const additionalDetailsHeading =
    findHeading({
      selector:
        '.ms-additional-details__title',
      text: 'Additional details',
    });

  if (additionalDetailsHeading) {
    const aN = safeText(
      additionalDetailsHeading,
    );

    $$('.ms-details__grid a', additionalDetailsHeading.closest('.ms-additional-details__container'),)
      .forEach((link) => {
        const subTopic = safeText(
          $(
            '.ms-details__content > h3',
            link.closest('.ms-details__item'),
          ),
        );

        updateAnalytics(link, {
          aN,
          cN: `${subTopic}_${safeText(link)}`,
        });
      });
  }

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 24. FAQ Top Buttons */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const faqTopSection = $('.ms-faq-section .ms-faq-section__top');

  const faqTitle = faqTopSection
    ? safeText($('h2', faqTopSection))
    : 'Frequently Asked Questions';

  if(faqTopSection){
    $$('.ms-button-group > button', faqTopSection).forEach((button) => {
      updateAnalytics(button, {
        aN: faqTitle,
        cN: safeText(button),
      });
    });
  }

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 25 / 26 FAQ Disclosure */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  $$('.ms-disclosure-group .ms-disclosure')
    .forEach((disclosure) => {
      const button = $('button.ms-disclosure__trigger', disclosure);

      const subTopic = safeText($('.ms-disclosure__label', button));

      bindExpandableAnalytics({
        button,
        aN: faqTitle,
        subTopic,
      });

      $$('.ms-disclosure__panel .ms-disclosure__content a', disclosure,).forEach((link) => {
        updateAnalytics(link, {
          aN: `${faqTitle}_${subTopic}`,
          cN: safeText(link),
          withTarget: true,
        });
      });
    });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 27. Social Links */
  /* ---------------------------- */
  const msSocialLockup = $('.ms-social__container .ms-social .ms-social__lockup')
  if(msSocialLockup){
    const aN = safeText($('.ms-social__text', msSocialLockup))
    $$('.ms-social__list-item > a', msSocialLockup)
      .forEach((link) => {
        const social =
          detectSocialMedia(link);

        updateAnalytics(link, {
          aN: aN,
          cN: social
            ? `${social}_CTA`
            : '',
          withTarget: true,
        });
      });

  }
};

/* -------------------------------- */
/* Session Detail Page */
/* -------------------------------- */
const setTaggingInLinkSpeakerAndModalElements = (speakerLinks = []) => {
  if(speakerLinks.length > 0){
    speakerLinks.forEach((speakerButton) => {
      updateAnalytics(speakerButton, {
        aN: 'Speaker',
        cN: safeText(speakerButton),
      });

      speakerButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
        let modalWasFound = false;
        const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
          const modal = $('.modal-dialog .modal-content');
          if (!modal) {
            // if it existed before and now it doesn't => it was closed
            if (modalWasFound) {
              observer.disconnect();
            }

            return;
          }

          modalWasFound = true;

          const speakerLink = $('.modal-dialog .modal-content .speaker-info a.see-all-sessions-btn')
          const speakerLinkText = `${safeText($('span.mdBtnR-text', speakerLink))}`
          if (speakerLink){
            updateAnalytics(speakerLink, {
              aN: 'Speaker',
              cN: speakerLinkText,
              withTarget: true,
            });
          }

          const closeButton = $('.modal-dialog .modal-content button.modal-close');
          const speakerNameH3 = $('.modal-dialog .modal-content .speaker-info h3.speaker-full-name');
          if(closeButton){
            updateAnalytics(closeButton, {
              aN: 'Speaker',
              cN: `${safeText(speakerNameH3)}_Close`,
            });
          }

          const socialMediaLinks = $$('.modal-dialog .modal-content .speaker-info ul.social-media-links li.social-media-link > a');
          if(socialMediaLinks.length > 0){
            socialMediaLinks.forEach((socialMediaLink) => {
              updateAnalytics(socialMediaLink, {
                aN: 'Speaker',
                cN: `${safeText(speakerNameH3)}_${detectSocialMedia(socialMediaLink)}`,
                withTarget: true
              });
            })
          }
        })

        observer.observe(document.body, {
          childList: true,
          subtree: true,
        });
      })
    })
  }
}

const addTaggingToCancelAndRemoveSession = (GUID) => {
  const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
    const cancelButton = $('.modal-dialog .modal-content button.cancel')
    if(!cancelButton) return;

    updateAnalytics(cancelButton, {
      aN: 'Session Details',
      cN: `Cancel_${GUID}`,
    });

    const removeSessionButton = $('.modal-dialog .modal-content button.confirm')
    if(removeSessionButton){
      updateAnalytics(removeSessionButton, {
        aN: 'Session Details',
        cN: `Yes, Remove Session_${GUID}`,
      });
    }

    const closeButton = $('.modal-dialog .modal-content button.modal-close');
    if(closeButton){
      updateAnalytics(closeButton, {
        aN: 'Session Details',
        cN: `Close_${GUID}`,
      });
    }

    observer.disconnect();
  });

  observer.observe(document.body, {
    childList: true,
    subtree: true,
  });
}

const handleSessionDetailButtons = (GUID, parent = document) => {
  const buttons = $$('.webinar-player-info .webinar-info-actions button, .session-actions button.session-action', parent)

  if(buttons.length > 0){
    buttons.forEach((button) => {
      button.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => {

        if(button.classList.contains('session-schedule')){
          updateAnalytics(button, {
            aN: 'Session Details',
            cN: `Add to Schedule_${GUID}`,
          });
        }

        if(button.classList.contains('session-scheduled')){
          // this button open a modal within buttons like [ Cancel, Yes remove Session]
          updateAnalytics(button, {
            aN: 'Session Details',
            cN: `Scheduled_${GUID}`,
          });
          // logic to handle the buttons in the modal
          addTaggingToCancelAndRemoveSession(GUID)
        }

        if(button.getAttribute('data-analytics-name') === 'favorited-webinar-info-interest'){
          updateAnalytics(button, {
            aN: 'Session Details',
            cN: `${GUID}_Remove from Favorite`,
          });
        }

        if(button.getAttribute('data-analytics-name') === 'add-to-favorites-webinar-info-interest'){
          updateAnalytics(button, {
            aN: 'Session Details',
            cN: `${GUID}_Add to Favorite`,
          });
        }
      }, true)
    });
  }
  // Section in the details session page color purple
  const container =  $('.webinar-player-info')
  if(container){
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      const button = $('.webinar-info-actions button.session-action')
      if(button.classList.contains('session-scheduled')){
        updateAnalytics(button, {
          aN: 'Session Details',
          cN: `Scheduled_${GUID}`,
        });
        addTaggingToCancelAndRemoveSession()
      }
      if(button.classList.contains('session-schedule')){
        updateAnalytics(button, {
          aN: 'Session Details',
          cN: `Add to Schedule_${GUID}`,
        });
      }

      if(button.getAttribute('data-analytics-name') === 'favorited-webinar-info-interest'){
        updateAnalytics(button, {
          aN: 'Session Details',
          cN: `${GUID}_Remove from Favorite`,
        });
      }

      if(button.getAttribute('data-analytics-name') === 'add-to-favorites-webinar-info-interest'){
        updateAnalytics(button, {
          aN: 'Session Details',
          cN: `${GUID}_Add to Favorite`,
        });
      }

    });

    observer.observe(container, {
      childList: true,
      subtree: true,
    });
  }
}

const handleSessionDetailPage = () => {
  // const pageTitle = safeText($('.keep-bullets h1'));
  const pageTitle = 'Session Details'
  const GUID = data?.page || ''

  const backToEvent = $('.keep-bullets a');

  updateAnalytics(backToEvent, {
    aN: pageTitle,
    cN: safeText(backToEvent),
    withTarget: true,
  });

  const backToCatalog = $('.back-to-catalog-container a');

  updateAnalytics(backToCatalog, {
    aN: pageTitle,
    cN: backToCatalog?.innerText?.trim(),
    withTarget: true,
  });

  handleSessionDetailButtons(GUID)

  // Set Tagging to Speaker Links
  const speakerLinks = $$('.session-participants .pictured-user-profile .profile-container button.speaker-trigger')
  if(speakerLinks.length > 0){
    setTaggingInLinkSpeakerAndModalElements(speakerLinks)
  }
};

/* -------------------------------- */
/* Sessions Page */
/* -------------------------------- */

const handleSessionsPage = () => {
  const sectionTitle = safeText($('.search-content h2.section-group-title'));

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 15 / 16 Session + Favourite */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const setFavoriteTagging = (button, isFavorite, GUID) => {
    updateAnalytics(button, {
      aN: 'Session',
      cN: `${GUID}_${!isFavorite? 'Add to Favorite' : 'Remove from Favorite'}`
    });
  };

  const tagsContainer = $('.search-container .rf-facets.rf-tag-container');
  if(tagsContainer){
    const applyTagging = () => {
      const tags = $$('button.rf-tag', tagsContainer);
      if(tags.length > 0){
        tags.forEach((tag) => {
          const tagText = safeText(tag.childNodes[0]).replace(/"/g, '');
          updateAnalytics(tag, {
            aN: 'Speaker',
            cN: `${tagText}_Remove filter`,
          });
        });
      }
    }

    applyTagging();

    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      applyTagging();
    });

    observer.observe(tagsContainer, {
      childList: true,
      subtree: true,
    });
  }

  $$('.catalog-result-title a').forEach(
    (link) => {
      const sessionTitle = safeText($('.title-text', link));

      const li = link.closest('li.catalog-result');
      const GUID = li.getAttribute('data-session-id') || '';

      const aN = `${sectionTitle}`;

      updateAnalytics(link, {
        aN,
        cN: sessionTitle,
        withTarget: true,
      });

      // Speakers button links in cards
      const speakerButtons = $$('.speakers-component .speaker-details p > button.speaker-trigger', li);
      if(speakerButtons.length > 0){
        setTaggingInLinkSpeakerAndModalElements(speakerButtons)
      }

      // Replay Webinar
      const sessionId = li.getAttribute('data-session-id') || '';
      const replayWebinarButton = getAnchorTagByText('Replay Webinar', li);
      if(replayWebinarButton){
        updateAnalytics( replayWebinarButton, {
          aN: 'Session Details ',
          cN: `Replay Webinar_${sessionId}`,
          withTarget: true,
        });
      }

      // 16. Set Favorite
      const favoriteButton = $('button.favorite-session', li);
      if(favoriteButton){
        const isFavorite = favoriteButton.classList.contains('favorited');
        setFavoriteTagging(favoriteButton, isFavorite, sessionId);

        favoriteButton.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => {
            const isFavorite = favoriteButton.classList.contains('favorited');
            setFavoriteTagging(favoriteButton, isFavorite, sessionId);
          },
          true
        );
      }

      handleSessionDetailButtons(GUID, li)

    },
  );

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 18. Clear Filters */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const clearButton = $('.search-filters .filters-header button[data-analytics-name="clear"]');

  updateAnalytics(clearButton, {
    aN: 'Filters',
    cN: safeText(clearButton),
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 19 / 20 Search */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const searchButton = $('.search-bar button[data-test="rf-button-search"]');

  const searchInput = $('.search-bar input[data-test="rf-text-input-node-search"]');

  if (searchButton && searchInput) {
    const updateSearchAnalytics = (value = '') => {
      updateAnalytics(searchButton, {
        aN: 'Upcoming Sessions_Search',
        cN: `Search_click_${value}`,
      });
    };

    updateSearchAnalytics(searchInput.value);

    searchInput.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
        updateSearchAnalytics(
          event.target.value,
        );
      },
    );

    searchInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter') {
          updateSearchAnalytics(
            event.target.value,
          );
        }
      },
    );
  }

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 21. Filters */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  $$('.filters-body ul.filters li > button',).forEach((button) => {
    const updateState = () => {
      const expanded =
        button.getAttribute(
          'aria-expanded',
        ) === 'true';

      updateAnalytics(button, {
        aN: 'Filters',
        cN: `${safeText(button)}_${
          expanded
            ? 'Expanded'
            : 'Collapsed'
        }`,
      });
    };

    updateState();

    button.addEventListener('click', () => {
        requestAnimationFrame(
          updateState,
        );
      },
    );
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* . Back to event */
  /* ---------------------------- */
  const backButton = getAnchorTagByText('Back to event')
  if(backButton){
    updateAnalytics(backButton, {
      aN: 'Sessions',
      cN: 'Back to event'
    });
  }

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* . Filters */
  /* ---------------------------- */
  const handleFiltersTagging = () => {
    const clearButton = $('.modal-footer .cancel, .filters-header button.clear-search');
    if(clearButton){
      updateAnalytics(clearButton, {
        aN: 'Filters',
        cN: 'Clear',
      });
    }

    const dayDropDown = $('ul.filters button.catalog-filter');

    if(dayDropDown){
      dayDropDown.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => {

          const expanded = dayDropDown.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
          updateAnalytics(dayDropDown, {
            aN: 'Filter',
            cN: `Day_${!expanded ? 'Expanded' : 'Collapsed'}`,
          });

          if(!expanded){
            const labelCheckboxes = $$('.filter-cat label.rf-checkbox');

            if(labelCheckboxes.length > 0){
              labelCheckboxes.forEach((label) => {
                label.addEventListener(
                  'pointerdown',
                  () => {
                    // requestAnimationFrame(() => {})
                    const input = label.querySelector('input[role="checkbox"]');
                    const checked = input.checked;
                    const text = safeText(label);
                    updateAnalytics(label, {
                      aN: 'Day',
                      cN: `${text}_${checked ? 'unselect' : 'select'}`,
                    });
                  },
                  true
                )
              })
            }
          }
        },
        true
      );
    }
  }
  handleFiltersTagging()
  const showFiltersButton = $('.search-actions > button')
  if(showFiltersButton){
    showFiltersButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        const modal = $('.modal-dialog .modal-content');

        if (!modal) return;

        handleFiltersTagging()
      });
    })
  }

};

/* -------------------------------- */
/* Digital Registration */
/* -------------------------------- */

const handleDigitalRegPage = () => {
  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 3. Privacy Links */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  [
    'Microsoft Privacy Statement',
    'GitHub Privacy Statement',
  ].forEach((text) => {
    const link =
      getAnchorTagByText(text);

    updateAnalytics(link, {
      aN: 'Register Now',
      cN: safeText(link),
      withTarget: true,
    });
  });

  /* ---------------------------- */
  /* 4. Continue Button */
  /* ---------------------------- */

  const continueButton = $('#wf-btn-submit');

  updateAnalytics(continueButton, {
    aN: 'Register Now',
    cN: safeText(continueButton),
  });
};

/* -------------------------------- */
/* Init */
/* -------------------------------- */

const PAGE_HANDLERS = {
  [PAGES.LANDING]: handleLandingPage,
  [PAGES.SESSIONS]: handleSessionsPage,
  [PAGES.DIGITAL_REG]: handleDigitalRegPage,
};

const init = () => {
  PAGE_HANDLERS[page]?.();

  if (isSessionDetailPage()) {
    handleSessionDetailPage();
  }
};

setTimeout(() => init(), 3000)

  </script>

  <script type="application/javascript">
    var Analytics = window.Rainfocus.Analytics;
    var Utils = window.Rainfocus.Utils;

    Analytics.addConfig((function () {
      var configId = '1789388027753009TaN4';
      var initScript = Utils.decodeEntities(document.getElementById('init_script_' + configId).innerHTML);
      var providerScript = Utils.decodeEntities(document.getElementById('provider_script_' + configId).innerHTML);
      var processScript = Utils.decodeEntities(document.getElementById('process_script_' + configId).innerHTML);
      
      var listenersScript = Utils.decodeEntities(document.getElementById('listeners_script_' + configId).innerHTML);
      

      var head = window.document.head;

      var savedDataForLaterRun = null;

      return {
        id: configId,
        initialized: false,
        type: '',
        init: function () {
          
          if (initScript.replace(/\s/g, ''))
            Utils.loadInlineScript(initScript, head);
          if (providerScript.replace(/\s/g, ''))
            Utils.loadInlineScript(providerScript, head);

          this.initialized = true;
          if (savedDataForLaterRun) {
            this.runProcessScript(savedDataForLaterRun);
            savedDataForLaterRun = null;
          }
          
          if (listenersScript.replace(/\s/g, ''))
            Utils.loadInlineScript(listenersScript, head);
          
        },
        runProcessScript: function (data) {
          if (this.initialized)
            Utils.executeScriptWithData(processScript, data);
          else
            savedDataForLaterRun = data;
        }
      }
    })());
  </script>
